Let's design a comprehensive algorithm:
- Maintain best sequence with exact score (via FFT or evaluator).
- We will run several stages over time:
  1. Generate diverse initial candidates: uniform, provided, frequency target variants, beta/exponential/cosine, random. Evaluate quickly.
  2. For each top candidate, run local optimization using smooth max (logsum-exp) / active max and maybe frequency target penalty. Use time budget.
  3. Use simulated annealing / block perturbations around best to refine.
- At end return best with exact evaluator verification.

Function `smooth_max_gradient(c, n, tau)`:
- M=2n-1
- z = c - c.max()
- w = np.exp(z/tau); w /= w.sum()
- weights sum1 length M.
- compute L = 1 << (M-1).bit_length() (same as score)
- Fp = np.fft.fft(p, n=L)
- Fw = np.fft.fft(w, n=L)
- A = np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
- B = np.fft.ifft(Fp * Fw).real[:n]
- h = A+B
- Center h: h -= h.mean() maybe.
Return h,w.

Exact target C:
```
def target_flat_spectrum(n, L):
    M=2*n-1
    k=np.arange(L)
    C=np.empty(L, dtype=complex)
    # Use formula: B = (1 - exp(-2j*pi*m*ratio))/(1 - exp(-2j*pi*ratio)) for k multiples? For k=0, B=M.
    # To avoid overflow near denominator zero, use np.sinc formula for amplitude and phase? Use complex geometric for simplicity with mask.
    x = 2*np.pi*k/L
    denom = np.exp(-1j*x) - 1.0
    numer = np.exp(-1j*x*M) - 1.0
    C = (1.0/M) * np.where(denom != 0, numer/denom, M)
```
But for k close 0 denom small, division okay? Use `np.expm1` for stability:
`1 - exp(-i x M) = -expm1(-1j x M)`, `1 - exp(-i x) = -expm1(-1j x)`. B = expm1(-1j x M)/expm1(-1j x). For x=0 handle.
Use `np.expm1(1j*x)`. When x small, ratio -> M. For k where x near 2π integer only k=0 since k range 0..L-1. good.
C[0]=1.
But C = B/M should have |C|≤1, C[0]=1.
Need S^2=C. We can choose S = np.sqrt(C) perhaps. However C is DFT of real target c, so C_{L-k}=conj(C_k). Principal sqrt may not preserve. We'll enforce:

```

Need ensure `fast_score` is called many times; it's O(n log n). We'll write efficient:
```
def fast_convolution_square(p, L=None):
   n=p.size; M=2*n-1
   if L is None: L=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=L)
   c=C[:M]
   # Due to numerical negative, max unaffected
   return c
```

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(p0, time_limit, rng, verify_func=None):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n=p.size; L=...
    best=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    # make all positive
    p = p + 1e-10; p/=p.sum()
    # Parameters schedules
    start = time.monotonic()
    tau = 1.0/n
    eta = 0.2
    noise = 0.2
    no_improve_iters=0
    best_local_score=best_score
    while time<deadline:
        c = conv_square(p,L)
        score = 2*n*c.max()
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=score; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        else: no_improve += 1
        # weights
        z = c - c.max()
        # Prevent underflow: maybe tau dynamic
        w = np.exp(z/tau); w/=w.sum()
        Fw=fft(w,L)
        Fp=fft(p,L)
        h = 2*ifft(Fw*conj(Fp)).real[:n]
        # Is h nonnegative? Since w,p nonneg, h nonneg. Center may have negative.
        base = np.dot(p,h)
        h = h - base
        # Add random noise with zero weighted mean
        if noise>0:
            nz = rng.normal(0,1,n)
            nz = nz - np.dot(p,nz)
            h = h - noise*nz  # minus because log shift = -eta*h + noise? We'll fold.
        # exp update
        step = eta
        log_change = -step*h
        log_change = np.clip(log_change, -20, 20)
        p_new = p * np.exp(log_change)
        p_new = np.clip(p_new, 1e-15, None)
        s=p_new.sum()
        if not finite or s<=0: p_new=p; p_new +=1e-10; p_new/=sum
        p = p_new/s
        # adapt tau, eta, noise
        if no_improve>100:
            # random jump
            if rng.random()<0.5:
                p = p * np.exp(0.5*(rng.normal(size=n)))
                p = np.clip(p,1e-12,None); p/=p.sum()
            tau *= 0.7; eta *=0.8; noise *=0.9
            no_improve=0
        else:
            tau = max(tau*0.999, 0.05/n?) etc
```

```
def _flat_box_spectrum(n, L):
    M=2*n-1
    k=np.arange(L)
    x = 2*np.pi*k/L
    # C = (1/M) * sum_{j=0}^{M-1} exp(-i x j)
    # Use expm1:
    ex = np.exp(-1j*x)
    exM = np.exp(-1j*x*M)
    denom = 1.0 - ex
    numer = 1.0 - exM
    C = np.empty(L, complex)
    mask = np.abs(denom) > 1e-14
    C[mask] = numer[mask]/(denom[mask]*M)
    C[~mask]=1.0
    C[0]=1.0
    return C
```
For x small but not zero, denominator small; expm1 better:
```
den = -np.expm1(-1j*x)
num = -np.expm1(-1j*x*M)
# where |den| small, ratio -> M
C = num/den/M
```
But `np.expm1` on complex array okay. For k=0, num=den=0. Use mask.
```
den = -np.expm1(-1j*x)
num = -np.expm1(-1j*x*M)
ratio = np.empty(L, complex)
mask = np.abs(den)>1e-8
ratio[mask]=num[mask]/den[mask]
ratio[~mask]=M
C=ratio/M
```
At k=L? not included. Good.

```
def _param_inits(n, rng):
  cands=[]
  # random logistic
  for t in range(5):
     x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
     mu=rng.uniform(-0.2,1.2); steep=rng.uniform(2,20)
     p=1/(1+np.exp(-steep*(x-mu)))
     # maybe multiply by envelope
     p=(p+1e-6); normalize
  # random beta skewed
     a=rng.uniform(0.3,5); b=rng.uniform(0.3,5)
     x clipped 1e-6,1-1e-6; p=x**(a-1)*(1-x)**(b-1)
  # random cosine wave squared
     r=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0); phi=rng.uniform(0,2π); m=rng.integers(1,5)
     p=1 + r*np.cos(m*(2π x/ n)+phi); p=p**2
  # random exponential
     lam=rng.uniform(-5,5); p=np.exp(lam*x)
```

Now core optimizer more detailed.

```
def _optimize_local(p0, seed, time_budget, target_score=1.5027, verbose=True):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=_normalize_positive(p0, eps=1e-15)
    n=p.size; L=_fft_len(n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score,c_best = _score_and_c(p,L)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    # Ensure all positive by adding small uniform
    p = p + 1e-12; p/=p.sum()
    # Parameter state
    beta_factor=1.0
    eta=0.5
    noise=0.2
    it=0
    last_best_time=start
    stagnations=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        score, c = _score_and_c(p,L)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best=p.copy(); best_score=score; last_best_time=time.monotonic(); stagnations=0
        else:
            stagnations += 1
        # if score not finite, reset to best
        if not np.isfinite(score): p=best.copy(); break?
        # weights
        beta_arg = c * (beta_factor / n)  # since c~1/n
        # stable softmax
        z = beta_arg - beta_arg.max()
        w = np.exp(z); wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum<=0 or not finite: w = np.full(2*n-1,1.0); w[-?]? no
        else: w/=wsum
        # cap weights optionally: use target C
        C_target = target_score/(2*n) if target_score>0 else existing
        # current max cmax = c.max()
        # cap_C = max(C_target, 0.9*cmax?) We want if max above target, weight excess.
        cap_C = max(C_target, 0.95*cmax)
        excess = np.maximum(c - cap_C, 0.0)
        esum=excess.sum()
        if esum>1e-300:
            excess/=esum
            cap_w = min(1.0, 0.5*beta_factor/10? maybe 0.5)
            w = (1-cap_w)*w + cap_w*excess
            w/=w.sum()
        # gradient
        h_raw = _corr_weighted(w,p,L)  # 2*crosscorr
        base = np.dot(p,h_raw)
        grad = n*(h_raw - base)
        # scale grad to avoid huge
        gnorm = np.sqrt(np.dot(grad,grad))
        if gnorm>1e6: grad/=gnorm
        # noise
        if noise>1e-4:
            nz = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            nz -= np.dot(p,nz)
            grad = grad + noise*nz
        # step
        if stagnations > 2000: # random larger noise
            if rng.random()<0.5:
                grad = grad + 1.0*rng.normal(n) - np.dot(p, rng.normal(n))
            beta_factor *= 0.7
            eta *= 0.8
            noise *= 0.9
            stagnations=0
        else:
            # anneal
            beta_factor = min(200.0, beta_factor*1.001)
            eta *= 0.999
            noise *= 0.999
        delta = -eta*grad
        delta = np.clip(delta, -20, 20)
        logp = np.log(p)
        logp += delta
        # re-center logp? Since delta included weighted mean zero? We used grad centered, noise zero weighted approx.
        # To improve stability, subtract weighted mean of delta again?
        # p_new = exp(logp), normalize.
        p_new = np.exp(logp - maybe max?) If logp large? p normalized logp~ -log n ~ -8; delta small; okay.
        p_new = np.clip(p_new, 1e-15, None)
        s=p_new.sum()
        if s<=0 or not finite: p_new=best.copy()
        else: p_new/=s
        p=p_new
        it+=1
        # periodic exact verify? optional
        if it % 2000 == 0 and time - last_best > 30:
            exact=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
            if exact < best_score? exact score corresponds maybe slightly but if finite and < current exact best? We don't track exact. Use if exact < best_score - 1e-9: best_score=exact; best=p.copy()
    # final exact verify best maybe outside
    return best, best_score
```

The SOTA LP might improve sequences significantly. We can approximate LP via multiplicative weights for dual as mentioned, lower cost. Let's explore this as an alternative update.

Given current f (p), M_c = max conv(f,f). We want find g≥0 maximizing sum g subject to conv(f,g) ≤ M_c (maybe after normalization). Since scale invariant, if f sum=1, M_c=C. Then rescale g sum1. If sum_g >1, g has higher sum under same M with f, mixing may reduce C. The dual: min C ∑ y_k s.t. h_i=∑_{a} f_a y_{a+i} ≥1, y≥0. We can solve dual with projected gradient/multiplicative weights:
- variables y length M nonnegative.
- objective ∑ y (C? irrelevant) with constraints h≥1. This is covering LP. Use soft objective: `L = sum(y) + (ρ/2)∑ max(0,1-h)^2`. Gradient wrt y_k =1 + ρ ∑_{i: a+i=k} f_a * max(0,1-h_i) (since h_i linear in y). This is cross-correlation similar! For fixed f, gradient evaluate h = crosscorr(f,y) (where h_i=∑_a f_a y_{a+i}) with FFT. We can minimize L using projected gradient/multiplicative. Then primal g can be recovered: at optimum, g_i>0 where h_i=1. Could set g_i ∝ max(0, 1 - h_i?) no. But we can run primal-dual: update y for constraints h<1, update g for constraints conv(f,g)>M. This is like Frank-Wolfe.

Maybe simpler: Solve primal with constraints using multiplicative weights: We maximize sum g. Equivalent minimize -sum g s.t. c_k=conv(f,g)≤M. Use log barrier: `L = -∑g + (ρ/2)∑ max(0, c_k-M)^2 + barrier`. Gradient wrt g_i = -1 + ρ ∑_{k excess} (c_k-M) f_{k-i}. Iterate:
- g_i ← g_i * exp(η (1 - ∑_k A_{k,i} y_k)) (ascent: if dual constraint slack ∑A y <1, increase g)
- y_k ← y_k * exp(η (A g_k / C - 1)) (if primal constraint violation A g_k >C, increase dual)
Normalize to keep sum. This is a standard multiplicative weight update for LP / game. It might find near optimum. Then use extreme primal.

Detailed function signatures:

```
def _as_1d_float(seq):
    try: arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64)
    except: return None
    if arr.ndim!=1 or arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0) # Actually evaluator clamps negatives, caps 1000.
    arr=np.where(arr<0,0,arr)
    arr=np.where(arr>1000,1000,arr)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<0.01: return None
    arr=arr/s  # normalize for search
    return arr
```
But if global has large values 1000, normalize. Good.

`_normalize(p, eps=1e-300)`: `p=np.asarray(p,float); p[p<0]=0; s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return uniform; p/=s; return p`.

`_score_and_c(p, L)`:
```
n=p.size; M=2*n-1
F=np.fft.fft(p, L)
c=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:M]
# Clean tiny imaginary/negatives
if c.size:
   maxc=c.max()
   # If maxc <=0 due underflow? set inf
   if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <=0:
       return np.inf, c
   return float(2.0*n*maxc), c
```
Using full FFT. We can use `np.fft.rfft` for score without c? But need c. Full ifft returns complex; real. For real convolution, using full ifft of F*F yields correct. `np.fft.irfft` requires rfft; but F*F from full? Could use full ifft. okay.

`_corr_weighted(w, p, L)`:
```
Fp=np.fft.fft(p, L)
Fw=np.fft.fft(w, L)
h=np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(Fp)).real[:p.size]
return 2.0*h
```

`_softmax_weights(c, beta_factor, n, cap_C=None, cap_weight=0.0)`:
```
arg = c * (beta_factor / n)
z = arg - arg.max()
w = np.exp(z)
s=w.sum()
if not finite or s<=0:
    w = np.full(c.size, 1/c.size)
else: w/=s
if cap_C is not None and cap_weight>0:
    excess = np.maximum(c - cap_C, 0.0)
    es=excess.sum()
    if es > 0:
        excess/=es
        w = (1-cap_weight)*w + cap_weight*excess
        w/=w.sum()
return w
```
`cap_weight` can be 0.3.

`_mult_update(p, grad, noise, eta, rng, eps)`? We'll implement inside `_optimize_local` for access. But top-level helper okay:
```
def _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, noise, eta, n, rng, eps):
    base = np.dot(p,h_raw)
    grad = float(n)*(h_raw - base)
    if noise>0:
       nz=rng.normal(size=p.size); nz -= np.dot(p,nz)
       grad = grad + noise*nz
    logp=np.log(np.maximum(p, eps))
    logp = logp - eta*grad
    logp = np.clip(logp, -700, 700)
    q=np.exp(logp)
    q=np.maximum(q, eps)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return p
    return q/s
```
But if we add noise to grad, after centering h, noise weighted mean zero? We compute nz missing dot? We do `nz -= np.dot(p,nz)`; p sum1. Good.

```
def _fast_score_only(p):
    p=_normalize(p)
    n=p.size; L=_fft_len(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,n=L)
    maxc=float(c[:2*n-1].max())
    return float(2*n*maxc)
```

Frequency init function:
```
def _freq_init(n, L_factor=4.0, exact=True):
    M=2*n-1
    L=1
    target = int(n*L_factor)
    L = 1 << max(0, (target-1).bit_length())  # power >= target
    # ensure L >= M maybe; target likely >
    if L < M: L = 1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    # target spectrum
    k=np.arange(L)
    x=2*np.pi*k/L
    den = -np.expm1(-1j*x)
    num = -np.expm1(-1j*x*M)
    ratio = np.ones(L, dtype=complex)
    mask = np.abs(den) > 1e-12
    ratio[mask] = num[mask]/den[mask]
    C = ratio/M
    C[0]=1.0
    # ensure Hermitian? C from formula should. But small errors.
    amp = np.abs(C)
    if exact:
        ph = 0.5*np.angle(C)
        # At zeros, angle 0; okay.
        S = np.sqrt(amp) * np.exp(1j*ph)
    else:
        ph = -np.pi*(n-1)*k/L
        S = np.sqrt(amp) * np.exp(1j*ph)
    # enforce hermitian
    half=L//2
    for kk in range(1, half+1):
        S[L-kk] = np.conj(S[kk])
    S[0] = 1.0 + 0j
    if L%2==0:
        # S[half] real. Use its real part; if exact C[half] positive, okay.
        S[half] = S[half].real + 0j
    p_raw = np.fft.ifft(S).real
    # generate windows
    candidates=[]
    starts=[0]
    if L>n:
        starts.append(max(0, (L-n)//2))
        starts.append(max(0, (L-n)//4))
    for st in starts:
        if st+n <= L:
            seg = p_raw[st:st+n].copy()
        else:
            a=L-st; seg=np.concatenate((p_raw[st:], p_raw[:n-a]))
        for shift in [0.0]:
            q = seg.copy()
            if shift:
                q = q - q.min()
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            if q.sum() > 0:
                q/=q.sum()
                candidates.append(q)
        # shifted version
        if np.min(seg) < 0:
            q2 = seg - np.min(seg)
            q2 = np.maximum(q2,0)
            if q2.sum()>0: q2/=q2.sum(); candidates.append(q2)
    if not candidates: candidates.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # choose best among windows based on score? We'll just add all to candidate list; scoring outside. But many. Limit to top 2 by quick internal? Could score here to reduce candidate list. But _fast_score cost maybe okay. To avoid too many, select best by a proxy: -negative sum? Better score.
    # We can score each with _fast_score_only and return best; but candidate generation wants multiple. Return list.
    return candidates
```
This returns multiple arrays. Need manage time; freq init for n=8192 L=65536: loops ok. Could be a bit slow but fine.

```

Now random inits. We'll implement `_random_param_inits(n, rng, count)`:
```
def _random_param_inits(n, rng, count=3):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    for _ in range(count):
        kind=int(rng.integers(0,4))
        if kind==0:
            mu=float(rng.uniform(-0.2,1.2)); s=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
            p=np.exp(-0.5*((x-mu)/s)**2)
        elif kind==1:
            a=float(rng.uniform(0.5,5.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(0.5,5.0))
            xc=np.clip(x,1e-6,1-1e-6)
            p=xc**(a-1)*(1-xc)**(b-1)
        elif kind==2:
            r=float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)); f=int(rng.integers(1,6)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
            p=1.0 + r*np.cos(f*(2*np.pi*x+ph))
            p=p*p
        else:
            lam=float(rng.uniform(-5,5)); p=np.exp(lam*x)
        p=np.maximum(p,0)
        if p.sum()>0: p/=p.sum(); out.append(p)
    return out
```
Also block random binary? Maybe not.

Resampling provided:
```
def _resample_sequence(p_old, n):
    n_old=p_old.size
    if n_old==n: return p_old.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n_old, dtype=float)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0, n_old-1, n)
    vals=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p_old)
    vals=np.maximum(vals,0)
    s=vals.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    return vals/s
```
If n larger, linear interpolation smooth.

Spectral objective gradient with C_target:
```
def _spectral_gradient(p, L, C):
    S = np.fft.fft(p, L)
    e = S*S - C
    H = S * np.conj(e)
    grad = (4.0 / L) * np.fft.fft(H).real[:p.size]
    return grad
```

Implement `_multiplicative_update`:
```
def _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta, noise, rng, eps):
    n=p.size
    base = float(np.dot(p,h_raw))
    grad = float(n)*(h_raw - base)
    if noise > 0:
       nz = rng.normal(size=n)
       nz -= float(np.dot(p,nz))
       grad = grad + noise*nz
    gscale = np.max(np.abs(grad))
    if gscale > 0:
       eta_eff = eta * min(2.0, 2.0/fmax(gscale,1e-12))
    else: eta_eff=0
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, eps))
    delta = -eta_eff*grad
    delta = np.clip(delta, -5.0, 5.0)
    # ensure weighted mean zero? delta already from centered grad+zero noise; clip may reintroduce.
    delta -= float(np.dot(p, delta))
    logp += delta
    q = np.exp(logp)
    q = np.maximum(q, eps)
    s = q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return p
    return q/s
```
Using `fmax`? Need import math? We can avoid; use `if gscale <1e-12`. `fmax` from math; can import math. Use `max`.
Potential issue: `np.max(np.abs(grad))` if grad nan. Check finite: if not finite, return p.
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): return p
```
But full finite check O(n). okay. Or use `np.isfinite(gscale)`.

Add `eps=1e-300`? If p has min 1e-300 log -690; after delta maybe -695 exp underflow? `np.exp(-695)=8e-302 okay; -700 underflow. eps=1e-300 safe. But if p clipped to 1e-300 many times, sum min contributions n*1e-300 negligible. Good.

Need ensure `rng.normal(size=n)` for large n many times; okay. If no noise, skip.

Now, `_score_c_fp(p,L)`:
```
def _score_c_fp(p,L):
    n=p.size; M=2*n-1
    S=np.fft.fft(p,n=L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(S*S,n=L).real[:M]
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc<=0:
       return np.inf,c,S
    return float(2.0*n*maxc), c, S
```
If c has negative tiny, max positive. Good.

def _collect_candidates(seed, target_lengths, provided):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands=[]
    # add provided and resampled
    for n in target_lengths:
       # uniform
       _add(cands, np.ones(n)/n, 'uniform')
       if provided is not None:
           if provided.size==n: p=provided.copy()
           else: p=_resample_sequence(provided,n)
           _add(cands,p,'provided')
       # freq inits
       if n <= 8192:
          for Lf in (4.0,8.0):
             for exact in (True, False):
                for p in _freq_init(n,Lf,exact): _add(cands,p,f'freq{Lf}{exact}')
       # random params 2
       for p in _random_param_inits(n,rng,2): _add(cands,p,'rand')
    # maybe add global best? none
    return cands
```

def _enforce_hermitian(arr):
    half=len(arr)//2
    # arr[1:half+1] -> arr[L-1:0:-1]
    if half >=1:
       arr[L-1:0:-1] = np.conj(arr[1:half+1])

    arr[0]=arr[0].real
    return arr

Let's implement `_make_hermitian(arr)`:
```
def _make_hermitian(a):
    L=a.size
    if L <=1:
       a[0]=a[0].real if L else None? return
    if L % 2 == 0:
       half = L//2
       if half > 1:
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half])
       if half >=0:
          a[half] = a[half].real + 0j
    else:
       half = (L-1)//2
       a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half+1])? Need odd not used.
    a[0]=a[0].real+0j
    return a
```

def _lp_refine(p, target_C, time_budget, seed):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    f=p.copy(); best=f; best_score=fast_score
    n=...
    C_current=best_score/(2*n)
    C_list=[max(target_C*1.05, C_current*0.9), max(target_C*1.05, C_current*0.8), C_current]
    for C in C_list:
       if time>deadline-5: break
       iters = min(120, max(30, int(time_budget*2))) # e.g. 120
       g=_lp_direction(f,C,iters=iters,eta=0.25)
       for t in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          if time>deadline: break
          q=(1-t)*f+t*g; q=_normalize
          s=fast_score_only(q)
          if s<best_score: best=q; best_score=s; f=q
    return best,best_score
```

1. `_fft_len(n)`:
```
def _fft_len(n):
    M = 2*int(n)-1
    if M <=1: return 1
    L = 1 << ((M-1).bit_length())
    return L
```
Check M=2? n=1 M=1 ->1; n=2 M=3, (2).bit_length=2 ->4. Good. For M=4 (n=2.5 none), M=5 n=3, (4).bit_length=3 ->8. Good.

2. `_normalize_positive(p, eps=1e-300)`:
```
def _normalize_positive(p, eps=1e-300):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)/256.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    a /= s
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    a /= a.sum()
    return a
```
If p has many eps after clip, sum slightly >1; fine. Use uniform size same? If invalid, return uniform length 256 might not match expected? Only for fallback. If p valid but all zero, return uniform same size. Good.

4. `_fast_score_only(p, L=None)`:
```
def _fast_score_only(p, L=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n ==0: return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
    p = p/s
    if L is None: L=_fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=L)
    cmax = float(np.max(C[:2*n-1]))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```
It normalizes p each time; if p from _normalize already, extra sum. okay.

5. `_score_c_fp(p,L)`: full as above.
Need if p not normalized? Ensure. It can assume normalized.

6. `_flat_target_spectrum(n,L)`:
```
def _flat_target_spectrum(n, L):
    M = 2*n-1
    k = np.arange(L)
    x = 2.0*np.pi*k/float(L)
    den = -np.expm1(-1j*x)
    num = -np.expm1(-1j*x*M)
    ratio = np.empty(L, dtype=np.complex128)
    ratio.fill(M)
    mask = np.abs(den) > 1e-12
    ratio[mask] = num[mask] / den[mask]
    C = ratio / float(M)
    C[0] = 1.0 + 0j
    _make_hermitian(C)
    # ensure nonnegative? C complex with magnitude; okay.
    return C
```
`ratio.fill(M)` where M int, complex? okay. At mask true near k=0 but den small might be inaccurate? Use threshold 1e-8 maybe to avoid division by very small causing error. For k=1, L large 65536, den~1e-4 >1e-8; okay. For L huge 1e6 not. Use 1e-10. Fine.

7. `_make_hermitian(a)`:
```
def _make_hermitian(a):
    L = a.size
    if L <=1:
       if L==1: a[0]=a[0].real+0j
       return a
    half = L//2
    if L % 2 == 0:
       if half > 1:
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half])
       if half >= 0:
          a[half] = a[half].real + 0j
    else:
       # not used but handle
       if half >= 1:
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half+1])? Need check. For odd L=5 half=2: pairs k=1->4, k=2->3. a[4:2:-1] indices4,3 length2; source a[1:3] indices1,2 length2. Good.
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half+1])
    a[0] = a[0].real + 0j
    return a
```
For even half>1 source `a[1:half]` excludes Nyquist; dest `a[L-1:half:-1]` indices L-1..half+1. Good. For L=2 half=1 no assignment, a[1]=real.

8. `_freq_init(n, L_factor=4.0, exact=True, max_windows=2)`:
Need use `_flat_target_spectrum(n,L)` (with L maybe large). `_fast_score_only` for selecting windows. It may score many. Implement.
```
def _freq_init(n, L_factor=4.0, exact=True, max_windows=2):
    M=2*n-1
    target = int(max(4.0, float(n)*float(L_factor)))
    L = 1 << max(0, (target-1).bit_length())
    if L < M: L = 1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    C = _flat_target_spectrum(n, L)
    amp = np.abs(C)
    k = np.arange(L)
    if exact:
       ph = 0.5*np.angle(C)
    else:
       ph = -np.pi*(n-1)*k/float(L)
    S = np.sqrt(amp) * np.exp(1j*ph)
    S[0]=1.0+0j
    _make_hermitian(S)
    p_raw = np.fft.ifft(S).real
    # candidate segments
    segs=[]
    starts=[0]
    if L > n:
       starts.append(max(0, (L-n)//2))
       starts.append(max(0, (L-n)//4))
    seen_starts=set()
    for st in starts:
       if st in seen_starts: continue
       seen_starts.add(st)
       if st+n <= L:
          seg = p_raw[st:st+n].copy()
       else:
          take = n - (L-st)
          seg = np.concatenate((p_raw[st:], p_raw[:take]))
       # positive
       q = np.maximum(seg,0.0)
       if q.sum() > 0: q/=q.sum(); segs.append(q)
       # shifted if negative
       mn = float(np.min(seg))
       if mn < 0:
          q2 = seg - mn
          q2 = np.maximum(q2,0.0)
          if q2.sum() >0: q2/=q2.sum(); segs.append(q2)
       # maybe global shift only negative min
    if not segs: segs.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # score and return best max_windows
    scored=[]
    for q in segs:
       s = _fast_score_only(q)
       if np.isfinite(s): scored.append((s,q))
    scored.sort(key=_cand_key_score)
    out=[scored[0][1]]
    if len(scored)>1: out.append(scored[1][1])
    return out
```
Need ensure no duplicate identical arrays? Could be; not critical.
`_cand_key_score` top-level.

Potential issue: `np.concatenate` when take=0? If st+n >L and L-st maybe <n. take positive because st <L. If st=0 and L<n impossible (L≥M≥n). Good.

12. `_spectral_gradient(p,L,C)`:
```
def _spectral_gradient(p, L, C):
    n=p.size
    S = np.fft.fft(p, n=L)
    e = S*S - C
    H = S * np.conj(e)
    grad = (4.0/float(L)) * np.fft.fft(H, n=L).real[:n]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return None? return np.zeros(n)
    return grad
```
Use `np.isfinite(gscale)` later; if not finite return zeros. To avoid large all-finite check, do `if np.max(np.abs(grad))` then finite. But if nan, max nan. `gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))`; if not finite return zeros. Return grad.
Cost: two FFTs. OK.

13. `_multiplicative_update` as above.
Need if `h_raw` contains nan: check.
```
def _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta, noise, rng, eps=1e-300):
    if h_raw is None: return p
    n=p.size
    if len(h_raw)!=n: return p
    base = float(np.dot(p,h_raw))
    if not np.isfinite(base): return p
    grad = float(n)*(h_raw - base)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): return p
    if noise>0:
       nz = rng.normal(size=n)
       nz -= float(np.dot(p,nz))
       grad = grad + noise*nz
    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
    if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale <= 1e-15:
       return p
    eta_eff = float(eta) * min(2.0, 2.0/gscale)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, eps))
    delta = -eta_eff*grad
    delta = np.clip(delta, -5.0, 5.0)
    delta -= float(np.dot(p, delta))
    logp += delta
    # Optional: if logp range too wide, shrink
    q = np.exp(logp)
    q = np.maximum(q, eps)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       return p
    return q/s
```
If grad all zero, no update. Good.
Noise parameter is added to grad before scaling; if noise large relative, random.

14. `_weights_from_c(c, beta, n, cap_C, cap_weight)`:
```
def _weights_from_c(c, beta, n, cap_C, cap_weight):
    M=c.size
    arg = c * (float(beta) * float(n))
    z = arg - float(np.max(arg))
    w = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <=0:
       w = np.full(M, 1.0/float(M))
    else: w/=ws
    if cap_weight and cap_C is not None:
       excess = np.maximum(c - float(cap_C), 0.0)
       es = float(excess.sum())
       if es > 0:
          excess/=es
          cw = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, cap_weight)))
          w = (1.0-cw)*w + cw*excess
          w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If beta large, arg max maybe inf? c finite, beta up to 500,n 8192 => arg ~500, exp okay. If n 2048, 500*2048*1e-3? ~1000, exp(1000) overflow? Wait arg = beta*n*c. c max ~1/n. beta=500 => arg ~500 (since n*c ~1). Actually n*c ~1, so arg~500. `z = arg - max` nonpositive, exp safe. If c has zeros, max 500. Good. No overflow because subtract max. But `arg` itself no exp. Fine.

```
def _optimize_from_start(p0, seed, time_budget, target_score=1.5027):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    n=p.size; L=_fft_len(n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score, _, _ = _score_c_fp(p,L)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
       best_score = np.inf
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(5.0, time_budget))
    last_best_time = time.monotonic()
    last_print = 0.0
    last_lp = time.monotonic()
    # spectral phase
    spec_time = min(60.0, 0.2*float(time_budget))
    if spec_time > 5.0:
       C_target = _get_target_C(n)[1]
       spec_deadline = time.monotonic() + spec_time
       it=0
       while time.monotonic() < spec_deadline:
           grad = _spectral_gradient(p,L,C_target)
           if grad is None: break
           h_raw = grad / float(n)
           p = _multiplicative_update(p,h_raw,eta=0.1,noise=0.05,rng=rng)
           if it % 20 == 19:
              score,c,S = _score_c_fp(p,L)
              if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                  best_score=score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic()
           it+=1
    start_main=time.monotonic()
    main_budget = max(1.0, (deadline - start_main))
    it=0; stagn=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        prog = min(1.0, (now - start_main)/main_budget)
        score, c, S = _score_c_fp(p,L)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            p = best.copy(); score = best_score; c,S = _score_c_fp(p,L)
        else:
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); stagn=0
            else:
                stagn += 1
        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30:
            print(f"  opt n={n} best={best_score:.6f} cur={score:.6f} prog={prog:.2f}", flush=True)
            last_print=time.monotonic()
        # parameters
        beta = 2.0 + 500.0*prog
        eta = 0.18*(1.0-0.7*prog) + 0.02
        noise = 0.12*(1.0-prog)**1.5
        cap_weight = 0.3 + 0.5*prog
        current_target = min(float(target_score), best_score*0.97 if np.isfinite(best_score) else target_score)
        if current_target < 1.1: current_target = 1.1  # maybe not too low
        target_C = current_target/(2.0*float(n))
        # lower floor average?
        floor_C = 0.55/float(n)
        if target_C < floor_C: target_C = floor_C
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else best_score/(2.0*n)
        cap_C = max(target_C, cmax*(0.75 - 0.45*prog))
        if cap_C < target_C*0.8: cap_C=target_C*0.8
        w = _weights_from_c(c, beta, n, cap_C, cap_weight)
        Fw = np.fft.fft(w, n=L)
        h_raw = 2.0 * np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(S), n=L).real[:n]
        p = _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta=eta, noise=noise, rng=rng)
        it += 1
        # stagnation/LP
        if time.monotonic() - last_best_time > 25.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            # either reset with noise or run LP
            if time.monotonic() - last_lp > 45.0:
                # run LP with small budget
                lp_budget = min(40.0, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.2)
                if lp_budget > 10.0:
                    p_lp, s_lp = _lp_refine(p, target_C, lp_budget, seed + 7919 + it)
                    if np.isfinite(s_lp) and s_lp < best_score:
                        best_score=s_lp; best=p_lp.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); stagn=0
                    p = p_lp.copy() if np.isfinite(s_lp) else best.copy()
                    last_lp = time.monotonic()
                else:
                    p = _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.5)
                    last_lp = time.monotonic()
            else:
                p = _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.3)
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()
            stagn=0
        if it % 2000 == 0: # optional exact? maybe too expensive. skip or if n<=4096
            pass
    # final exact? not here to allow multiple candidates? We can exact at end globally. But update best maybe.
    return best, best_score
```
Need `_random_jump` helper:
```
def _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.3):
    base = best if best is not None else p
    base = _normalize_positive(base)
    nz = rng.normal(size=base.size)
    nz -= float(np.dot(base,nz))
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300)) + np.clip(scale*nz, -3, 3)
    q=np.exp(logp); q=np.maximum(q,1e-300); s=q.sum(); return q/s if s>0 else base
```
Scale relative; okay.

def _get_target_C(n):
    if n in _TARGET_C_CACHE: return _TARGET_C_CACHE[n]
    L=_fft_len(n)
    C=_flat_target_spectrum(n,L)
    _TARGET_C_CACHE[n]=(L,C)
    return L,C

`_lp_refine` implementation:
```
def _lp_direction(f, C, iters=80, eta=0.25):
    n=f.size; L=_fft_len(n); M=2*n-1
    g=f.copy()
    c_f = _conv_square_rfft(f,L) # maybe define `_conv_square_rfft`
    # y init: excess over 0.8C
    y = np.maximum(c_f - 0.8*C, 0.0)
    ys = y.sum()
    if ys <=0: y = np.ones(M)/M
    else: y/=ys
    Ff = np.fft.fft(f, n=L)
    eps=1e-300
    for _ in range(iters):
        Fg = np.fft.fft(g, n=L)
        v = np.fft.ifft(Ff*Fg, n=L).real[:M]
        Fy = np.fft.fft(y, n=L)
        h = np.fft.ifft(Fy*np.conj(Ff), n=L).real[:n]
        # update g
        alpha = np.clip(1.0 - h, -2.0, 2.0)
        logg = np.log(np.maximum(g, eps)) + eta*alpha
        logg -= float(np.dot(g, np.clip(eta*alpha, -1,1)?))? To keep sum, just normalize after.
        gq = np.exp(np.clip(logg, -700, 700))
        gq = np.maximum(gq, eps)
        gs = gq.sum()
        if gs>0: g = gq/gs
        # update y
        beta = np.clip(v/float(max(C,1e-15)) - 1.0, -2.0, 2.0)
        logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, eps)) + eta*beta
        yq = np.exp(np.clip(logy, -700, 700))
        yq = np.maximum(yq, eps)
        ys = yq.sum()
        if ys>0: y = yq/ys
    return g
```
Need `_conv_square_rfft`:
```
def _conv_square_rfft(p,L):
    n=p.size; F=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L); return np.fft.irfft(F*F,n=L)[:2*n-1]
```
In `_lp_direction`, `Ff` full; for v using full Ff*Fg. Good. But Fy length M zero padded; full FFT.
The scaling of logg with alpha: If we just add and normalize, no need center alpha; but if alpha has positive mean, g all increases, normalized same. Normalization equivalent to subtract mean in log? Not exactly but okay. We can center alpha to reduce bias:
```
alpha -= float(np.dot(g, alpha))
beta -= float(np.dot(y, beta))
```
Do that.
Update log with eta*alpha centered.
`eta=0.25`, alpha maybe O1, delta 0.25.
Return g.

def _lp_refine(p, target_C, time_budget, seed):
    rng=...
    f=_normalize_positive(p)
    best=f.copy(); best_score=fast_score_only(f)
    n=...

    C_list=[max(target_C*1.05, C_current*0.9), max(target_C*1.1, C_current*0.8), C_current]
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget

       while time.monotonic()<deadline-5:
          # run one direction with 60 iters, maybe mix
          g=_lp_direction(f,max(C,1e-15), iters=80,eta=0.25)
          for t in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
             q=(1-t)*f+t*g; q/_normalize; s=fast_score_only
             if s<best: update f=q
          # stop if time low
          if time.monotonic() > deadline-15: break
    return best,best_score

def _collect_candidates(seed, lengths, provided):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands=[]
    for n in lengths:
        _add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")
        if provided is not None and provided.size > 0:
            if provided.size == n:
                p = provided.copy()
            else:
                p = _resample_sequence(provided, n)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, "provided")
        # freq inits
        if n <= 8192:
            for Lf in (4.0, 8.0):
                for exact in (True, False):
                    for p in _freq_init(n, Lf, exact):
                        _add_candidate(cands, p, f"freq_{Lf}_{'e' if exact else 'c'}")
        # random
        for p in _random_param_inits(n, rng, 2):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, "rand")
    return cands
```

def _select_candidates(cands, max_sel=6):
    if not cands: return []
    cands_sorted = sorted(cands, key=_cand_key_score)
    best_per_len = {}
    for item in cands_sorted:
       n = item[1].size
       if n not in best_per_len:
          best_per_len[n]=item
    selected=[]; ids=set()
    for item in best_per_len.values():
       selected.append(item); ids.add(id(item[1]))
    selected.sort(key=_cand_key_score)
    for item in cands_sorted:
       if len(selected)>=max_sel: break
       if id(item[1]) not in ids:
          selected.append(item); ids.add(id(item[1]))
    selected.sort(key=_cand_key_score)
    return selected
```

def _unique_candidates(items, limit):
   out=[]; ids=set()
   for s,p in items:
      if id(p) in ids: continue
      ids.add(id(p)); out.append((s,p.copy()))
      if len(out)>=limit: break
   return out

def _evaluate_safe(p):

Let's revisit gradient for main objective: `h_raw = 2*ifft(Fw*conj(S))`. This equals 2*crosscorr(w,p) where p current. Weight w sum1. `h_raw` nonnegative. base dot(p,h_raw) = 2∑_j p_j ∑_k w_k p_{k-j} = 2∑_k w_k ∑_j p_j p_{k-j} = 2∑_k w_k c_k = 2 weighted average c. `grad = n*(h_raw - base)`. This is consistent with gradient of ∑w c under simplex? derivative ∂∑w c/∂p_j = 2∑w p_{k-j}=h_raw. On simplex, project subtract weighted average. Good.

For cap weights normalized sum1. Good.

Could the objective be scale invariant and our p sum1; gradient of ∑w c with w depending on c not just linear. We compute weights from c then step; quasi-Newton/steepest. Fine.

Spectral gradient derivation: If p sum1, S0=1, e0=0. Objective O scale? If p normalized. Good.

Now, maybe `_multiplicative_update` with `h_raw` from spectral gradient where `h_raw=grad/n`, base dot p h_raw ~? grad centered? It will compute again base. Good.

Let's ensure `_spectral_gradient` returns gradient not scaled by n? It returns O(1). We set h_raw=grad/n. In update, grad_update = n*(h_raw - base) = grad - n*base. But base = dot(p,grad/n)=dot(p,grad)/n. n*base=dot(p,grad). So effective = grad - weighted mean. Good. So no need center in spectral gradient. Good.

Now, final sequence values: `_normalize_positive` enforces max 1000 and min 0. It clips values to 1000; normalized sum <=1 so max <=1. Fine. It sets min eps=1e-300 and renormalizes; sum exactly 1? The last normalization after maximum may change sum by eps*n, then divide again? In code:
```
a/=s
a=np.maximum(a, eps)
a/=a.sum()
```
Yes. Values min eps, sum1.
But setting every zero to eps adds ~n*1e-300 negligible.

Detailed code for `_make_hermitian`:
```
def _make_hermitian(a):
    L = a.size
    if L == 0: return a
    if L == 1:
       a[0] = a[0].real + 0j
       return a
    half = L//2
    if L % 2 == 0:
       if half > 1:
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half])
       a[half] = a[half].real + 0j
    else:
       if half > 0:
          a[L-1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half+1])
    a[0] = a[0].real + 0j
    return a
```
For L=2 half=1, half>1 false, a[1]=real. Pairs k=1 self. Good.
For L=4 half=2, assign a[3:2:-1] -> index3 from conj a[1]; a[2] real. Good.
For L=8 half=4, assign a[7:4:-1] indices7,6,5 from conj a[1],a[2],a[3]; a[4] real. Good.
For odd L=5 half=2, assign a[4:2:-1] indices4,3 from conj a[1],a[2]; a[0] real. Good.
Works.

`_flat_target_spectrum`: For L=8, M=7. k=0, den=0 mask false ratio=7 C=1. k=1 den~1e-4, num? good. `_make_hermitian` sets C[4]=real; M odd L even C_half should positive. Good.

`_freq_init`: `target = int(max(4.0, float(n)*float(L_factor)))`. If n*Lf is float, `max` with float. `L = 1 << max(0, (target-1).bit_length())`: if target=4, (3).bit_length=2 ->4. L≥M? if n=3 M=5, L=4<M, then next loop? We do `if L < M: L = 1 << (M-1).bit_length()`. Good.
`_flat_target_spectrum(n,L)` uses M=2n-1 ≤ L.
`S = np.sqrt(amp) * np.exp(1j*ph)`. If exact, ph=0.5*np.angle(C). At C[0]=1 ph0=0. For C negative? angle π, ph π/2; okay. Hermitian enforcement may flip.
`p_raw = np.fft.ifft(S).real`.

def _as_1d_float(seq):
   if seq is None: return None
   try:
      a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64)
   except Exception:
      return None
   if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: return None
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return None? But evaluator would inf. For resampling, if sum too low invalid. Return None.
   return a/s
```

`_lp_refine`:
```
def _lp_refine(p, target_C, time_budget, seed):
    rng=...
    f=_normalize_positive(p)
    best=f.copy(); best_score=_fast_score_only(f)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(5.0,time_budget)
    n=f.size
    C_current=best_score/(2*n) if finite else 1.0/n
    C_list=[max(target_C*1.05, C_current*0.9), max(target_C*1.1, C_current*0.8), C_current]
    # maybe random C lower
    while time.monotonic()<deadline-5:
       # choose C cycling
       for C in C_list:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline-5: break
          # estimate iterations: maybe 60 for large? Use 80.
          iters = 80 if n <=4096 else 50
          g=_lp_direction(f, C, iters=iters, eta=0.25)
          for t in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
             q=(1-t)*f+t*g
             q=_normalize_positive(q)
             s=_fast_score_only(q)
             if np.isfinite(s) and s<best_score:
                 best_score=s; best=q.copy(); f=q.copy()
          # after each direction, maybe if time low break
    return best,best_score
```
This may run many directions if time_budget 40 and iters 50 each maybe 0.5-1s; okay. But for n=8192, 8 directions 8s. fine.

`_random_jump`: Need not use `best` if None.
```
def _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.3):
    base = best if best is not None else p
    base = _normalize_positive(base)
    nz = rng.normal(size=base.size)
    nz -= float(np.dot(base,nz))
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))
    delta = np.clip(scale*nz, -3.0, 3.0)
    q = np.exp(logp + delta)
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return base
    return q/s
```
Scale 0.3 moderate. Good.

def _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.3):
    base = _normalize_positive(best if best is not None else p)
    mix = float(rng.uniform(0.0, max(0.0, scale)))
    if mix > 1e-6:
        r = np.exp(rng.normal(size=base.size))
        r = np.clip(r, 1e-9, None)
        r = r/r.sum()

        q=base

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
without relying on negative values or evaluator clipping.

The program combines:
1) flat-target frequency initialization (square-root of a flat convolution spectrum),
2) smooth max / capped-weight projected multiplicative gradient descent,
3) a lightweight primal-dual LP-style direction used for escapes,
4) multiple lengths and diverse random starts, with exact verification at the end.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

_EPS = 1e-12
_TARGET_C_CACHE = {}


def _fft_len(n):
    """Smallest power-of-two FFT length >= 2*n - 1."""
    M = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if M <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_positive(p, eps=_EPS):
    """Clip, normalize, and keep a strictly positive feasible search vector."""
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)

    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    return a


def _as_1d_float(seq):
    """Convert a stored sequence to normalized nonnegative floats, or None."""
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return None
    return a / s


def _fast_score_only(p, L=None):
    """Fast FFT-based score, no gradient data returned."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    if L is None:
        L = _fft_len(n)

    F = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(F * F, n=L)
    cmax = float(np.max(conv[:2 * n - 1]))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def _conv_square_rfft(p, L):
    n = int(p.size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    return np.fft.irfft(F * F, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]


def _score_c_fp(p, L):
    """Full-FFT score plus weights-friendly convolution coefficients and FFT."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    M = 2 * n - 1

    S = np.fft.fft(p, n=L)
    conv = np.fft.ifft(S * S, n=L).real[:M]
    cmax = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), conv, S
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax), conv, S


def _make_hermitian(a):
    """Enforce conjugate symmetry for a DFT-sized complex array."""
    L = int(a.size)
    if L == 0:
        return a
    if L == 1:
        a[0] = a[0].real + 0j
        return a

    half = L // 2
    if L % 2 == 0:
        if half > 1:
            a[L - 1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half])
        a[half] = a[half].real + 0j
    else:
        if half > 0:
            a[L - 1:half:-1] = np.conj(a[1:half + 1])
    a[0] = a[0].real + 0j
    return a


def _flat_target_spectrum(n, L):
    """
    DFT of the target linear-convolution coefficients:
        c_k = 1/(2n-1), k = 0,...,2n-2, zero-padded to length L.
    """
    M = 2 * int(n) - 1
    k = np.arange(L)
    x = 2.0 * np.pi * k / float(L)

    den = -np.expm1(-1j * x)
    num = -np.expm1(-1j * x * M)

    ratio = np.empty(L, dtype=np.complex128)
    ratio.fill(M)
    mask = np.abs(den) > 1e-12
    ratio[mask] = num[mask] / den[mask]

    C = ratio / float(M)
    C[0] = 1.0 + 0j
    _make_hermitian(C)
    return C


def _get_target_C(n):
    if n in _TARGET_C_CACHE:
        return _TARGET_C_CACHE[n]
    L = _fft_len(n)
    C = _flat_target_spectrum(n, L)
    _TARGET_C_CACHE[n] = (L, C)
    return L, C


def _reshape_power(q, alpha):
    """Return a normalized nonnegative power-smeared version of q."""
    if alpha == 1.0:
        return q.copy()
    p = np.power(np.maximum(np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64), 0.0), float(alpha))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return q.copy()
    return p / s


def _freq_init(n, L_factor=4.0, exact_phase=True, max_windows=2):
    """
    Initialize from square-root of the flat-box convolution spectrum.

    exact_phase=True uses principal square-root phases of the target spectrum.
    exact_phase=False uses a centered linear phase, then Hermitian correction.
    """
    M = 2 * int(n) - 1
    target = int(max(4.0, float(n) * float(L_factor)))
    L = 1 << max(0, (target - 1).bit_length())
    if L < M:
        L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()

    C = _flat_target_spectrum(n, L)
    amp = np.abs(C)
    k = np.arange(L)

    if exact_phase:
        ph = 0.5 * np.angle(C)
    else:
        ph = -np.pi * (n - 1) * k / float(L)

    S = np.sqrt(amp) * np.exp(1j * ph)
    S[0] = 1.0 + 0j
    _make_hermitian(S)

    p_raw = np.fft.ifft(S).real
    scored = []

    starts = [0]
    if L > n:
        starts.append(max(0, (L - n) // 2))
        starts.append(max(0, (L - n) // 4))

    seen = set()
    for st in starts:
        if st in seen or st >= L:
            continue
        seen.add(st)

        if st + n <= L:
            seg = p_raw[st:st + n].copy()
        else:
            take = n - (L - st)
            seg = np.concatenate((p_raw[st:], p_raw[:take]))

        base = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)
        bs = float(base.sum())
        candidates = []
        if bs > 0.0:
            candidates.append(base / bs)

        mn = float(np.min(seg))
        if mn < 0.0:
            shifted = seg - mn
            shifted = np.maximum(shifted, 0.0)
            ss = float(shifted.sum())
            if ss > 0.0:
                candidates.append(shifted / ss)

        for cand in candidates:
            candidates2 = [cand, _reshape_power(cand, 0.5), _reshape_power(cand, 2.0)]
            for c in candidates2:
                c = _normalize_positive(c)
                s = _fast_score_only(c)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    scored.append((s, c))

    if not scored:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    scored.sort(key=lambda item: item[0])
    out = [scored[0][1]]
    if len(scored) > 1:
        out.append(scored[1][1])
    return out[:max_windows]


def _random_param_inits(n, rng, count=2):
    """A few smooth parametric starts to escape poor basins."""
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))

    for _ in range(max(1, int(count))):
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
        if kind == 0:
            mu = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 1.2))
            sig = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5))
            p = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)
        elif kind == 1:
            a = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 5.0))
            b = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 5.0))
            xc = np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
            p = (xc ** (a - 1.0)) * ((1.0 - xc) ** (b - 1.0))
        elif kind == 2:
            r = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            f = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            p = 1.0 + r * np.cos(f * (2.0 * np.pi * x + ph))
            p = p * p
        else:
            lam = float(rng.uniform(-5.0, 5.0))
            p = np.exp(lam * x)

        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            out.append(p / s)

    return out


def _resample_sequence(p_old, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a sequence to a new length."""
    p_old = np.asarray(p_old, dtype=np.float64)
    n_old = int(p_old.size)
    if n_old <= 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if n_old == int(n):
        return _normalize_positive(p_old)

    x_old = np.arange(n_old, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(n_old - 1), int(n))
    vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p_old)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return vals / s


def _cand_key_score(item):
    return item[0]


def _item_score(item):
    return item[0]


def _add_candidate(cands, p, source):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    s = _fast_score_only(p)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        cands.append((float(s), p, source))


def _collect_candidates(seed, lengths, provided):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands = []

    for n in lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0:
            continue

        _add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")

        if provided is not None:
            if provided.size == n:
                p = provided.copy()
            else:
                p = _resample_sequence(provided, n)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, "provided")

        if 16 <= n <= 10000:
            for lf in (4.0, 8.0):
                for exact in (True, False):
                    for p in _freq_init(n, lf, exact):
                        _add_candidate(cands, p, f"freq_{lf}_{'e' if exact else 'c'}")

        for p in _random_param_inits(n, rng, 2):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, "rand")

    return cands


def _select_candidates(cands, max_sel=6):
    if not cands:
        return []

    cands_sorted = sorted(cands, key=_cand_key_score)

    best_per_len = {}
    for item in cands_sorted:
        n = item[1].size
        if n not in best_per_len:
            best_per_len[n] = item

    len_items = list(best_per_len.values())
    len_items.sort(key=_cand_key_score)

    selected = len_items[:max_sel]
    ids = set()
    for item in selected:
        ids.add(id(item[1]))

    for item in cands_sorted:
        if len(selected) >= max_sel:
            break
        if id(item[1]) not in ids:
            selected.append(item)
            ids.add(id(item[1]))

    selected.sort(key=_cand_key_score)
    return selected


def _unique_candidates(items, limit):
    items = sorted(items, key=_item_score)
    out = []
    ids = set()
    for s, p in items:
        pid = id(p)
        if pid in ids:
            continue
        ids.add(pid)
        out.append((float(s), np.array(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
        if len(out) >= limit:
            break
    return out


def _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta, noise, rng, eps=_EPS):
    """
    Simplex-preserving multiplicative step.
    h_raw should be of scale ~1/n so that n*(h_raw - weighted_mean) is O(1).
    """
    if h_raw is None:
        return p
    n = p.size
    if h_raw.size != n:
        return p

    base = float(np.dot(p, h_raw))
    if not np.isfinite(base):
        return p

    grad = float(n) * (h_raw - base)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return p

    if noise and noise > 1e-12:
        nz = rng.normal(size=n)
        nz -= float(np.dot(p, nz))
        grad = grad + float(noise) * nz

    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
    if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale <= 1e-15:
        return p

    eta_eff = float(eta) * min(2.0, 2.0 / gscale)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, eps))
    delta = -eta_eff * grad
    delta = np.clip(delta, -5.0, 5.0)
    delta -= float(np.dot(p, delta))
    logp += delta

    q = np.exp(logp)
    q = np.maximum(q, eps)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p
    return q / s


def _spectral_gradient(p, L, C):
    """
    Gradient of ||FFT(p)^2 - C||^2, where C is the flat-box spectral target.
    Scaled so that _multiplicative_update can consume grad/n as h_raw.
    """
    n = p.size
    S = np.fft.fft(p, n=L)
    e = S * S - C
    H = S * np.conj(e)
    grad = (4.0 / float(L)) * np.fft.fft(H, n=L).real[:n]

    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(gscale):
        return None
    return grad


def _weights_from_c(c, beta, n, cap_C, cap_weight):
    """
    Normalize coefficient weights: a softmax of c plus optional cap-excess mass.
    """
    M = c.size
    if M <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)

    arg = c * (float(beta) * float(n))
    z = arg - float(np.max(arg))
    w = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(M, 1.0 / float(M))
    else:
        w = w / ws

    if cap_weight and cap_C is not None:
        excess = np.maximum(c - float(cap_C), 0.0)
        es = float(excess.sum())
        if es > 1e-300:
            excess = excess / es
            cw = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, cap_weight)))
            w = (1.0 - cw) * w + cw * excess
            w = w / float(w.sum())
    return w


def _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.3):
    """Exploratory restart: mix with a random nonnegative vector and jitter."""
    base = best if best is not None else p
    base = _normalize_positive(base)
    n = base.size

    mix = float(rng.uniform(0.0, max(0.0, float(scale))))
    if mix > 1e-9:
        r = np.exp(rng.normal(size=n))
        r = np.clip(r, 1e-9, None)
        rs = float(r.sum())
        if rs > 0:
            r = r / rs
        else:
            r = np.ones(n) / float(n)
        q = (1.0 - mix) * base + mix * r
    else:
        q = base.copy()

    q = _normalize_positive(q)
    nz = rng.normal(size=q.size)
    nz -= float(np.dot(q, nz))
    logq = np.log(np.maximum(q, _EPS)) + np.clip(float(scale) * nz, -3.0, 3.0)
    qq = np.exp(logq)
    qq = np.maximum(qq, _EPS)
    s = float(qq.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return base
    return qq / s


def _lp_direction(f, C, iters=80, eta=0.25):
    """
    Lightweight primal-dual multiplicative-weight direction:
        primal max sum(g) s.t. conv(f,g) <= C, g>=0
        dual   min C sum(y) s.t. A^T y >= 1, y>=0
    Returns a normalized direction g.
    """
    n = f.size
    L = _fft_len(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    C = float(max(C, 1e-15))

    g = f.copy()
    c_f = _conv_square_rfft(f, L)
    y = np.maximum(c_f - 0.8 * C, 0.0)
    ys = float(y.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ys) or ys <= 0.0:
        y = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / float(M)
    else:
        y = y / ys

    Ff = np.fft.fft(f, n=L)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        Fg = np.fft.fft(g, n=L)
        v = np.fft.ifft(Ff * Fg, n=L).real[:M]

        Fy = np.fft.fft(y, n=L)
        h = np.fft.ifft(Fy * np.conj(Ff), n=L).real[:n]

        alpha = 1.0 - h
        alpha -= float(np.dot(g, alpha))
        alpha = np.clip(alpha, -2.0, 2.0)
        logg = np.log(np.maximum(g, _EPS)) + float(eta) * alpha
        logg = np.clip(logg, -700.0, 700.0)
        gq = np.exp(logg)
        gq = np.maximum(gq, _EPS)
        gs = float(gq.sum())
        if np.isfinite(gs) and gs > 0.0:
            g = gq / gs

        beta = np.clip(v / C - 1.0, -2.0, 2.0)
        beta -= float(np.dot(y, beta))
        logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, _EPS)) + float(eta) * beta
        logy = np.clip(logy, -700.0, 700.0)
        yq = np.exp(logy)
        yq = np.maximum(yq, _EPS)
        ys = float(yq.sum())
        if np.isfinite(ys) and ys > 0.0:
            y = yq / ys

    return _normalize_positive(g)


def _lp_refine(p, target_C, time_budget, seed):
    """Use LP-style directions and mixtures to escape local basins."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    f = _normalize_positive(p)
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_only(f)

    n = f.size
    if n <= 0:
        return best, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, float(time_budget))
    C_current = best_score / (2.0 * float(n)) if np.isfinite(best_score) else 1.0 / float(n)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        C_list = [
            max(float(target_C) * 1.05, C_current * 0.9),
            max(float(target_C) * 1.1, C_current * 0.8),
            C_current,
        ]

        for C in C_list:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break

            iters = 60 if n <= 4096 else 40
            g = _lp_direction(f, C, iters=iters, eta=0.25)

            for t in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                q = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
                q = _normalize_positive(q)
                s = _fast_score_only(q)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                    best_score = s
                    best = q.copy()
                    f = q.copy()
                    C_current = best_score / (2.0 * float(n))

        # If we have been here a while, occasionally jump from best.
        if rng.random() < 0.1:
            f = _random_jump(f, best, rng, scale=0.25)

    return best, best_score


def _optimize_from_start(p0, seed, time_budget, target_score=1.5027):
    """Monotone-ish local optimization with spectral pre-conditioning."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    n = p.size
    L = _fft_len(n)

    best = p.copy()
    best_score, _, _ = _score_c_fp(p, L)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, float(time_budget))
    last_best_time = time.monotonic()
    last_print = 0.0
    last_lp = time.monotonic()

    # Phase 1: target-spectrum correction.
    spec_time = min(60.0, 0.2 * float(time_budget))
    if spec_time > 5.0:
        C_target = _get_target_C(n)[1]
        spec_deadline = time.monotonic() + spec_time
        it = 0
        while time.monotonic() < spec_deadline:
            grad = _spectral_gradient(p, L, C_target)
            if grad is None:
                break
            h_raw = grad / float(n)
            p = _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta=0.10, noise=0.10, rng=rng)
            if (it % 20) == 19:
                score, _, _ = _score_c_fp(p, L)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best = p.copy()
                    last_best_time = time.monotonic()
            it += 1

    # Phase 2: smooth-max / capped weight gradient descent with escapes.
    start_main = time.monotonic()
    main_budget = max(1.0, deadline - start_main)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        prog = min(1.0, (now - start_main) / main_budget)

        score, c, S = _score_c_fp(p, L)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            p = best.copy()
            score, c, S = _score_c_fp(p, L)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = p.copy()
            last_best_time = now
        elif not np.isfinite(score):
            best_score = float("inf")

        if now - last_print > 30.0:
            print(f"  [optimize] n={n} best={best_score:.6f} current={score:.6f} prog={prog:.2f}", flush=True)
            last_print = now

        beta = 2.0 + 500.0 * prog
        eta = 0.18 * (1.0 - 0.7 * prog) + 0.02
        noise = 0.12 * ((1.0 - prog) ** 1.5)
        cap_weight = 0.3 + 0.5 * prog

        current_target = min(float(target_score), best_score * 0.97 if np.isfinite(best_score) else float(target_score))
        if current_target < 1.10:
            current_target = 1.10
        target_C = current_target / (2.0 * float(n))
        floor_C = 0.55 / float(n)
        target_C = max(target_C, floor_C)

        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else (best_score / (2.0 * float(n)) if np.isfinite(best_score) else 1.0 / float(n))
        cap_C = max(target_C, cmax * (0.75 - 0.45 * prog))

        w = _weights_from_c(c, beta, n, cap_C, cap_weight)
        Fw = np.fft.fft(w, n=L)
        h_raw = 2.0 * np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(S), n=L).real[:n]

        p = _multiplicative_update(p, h_raw, eta=eta, noise=noise, rng=rng)
        it += 1

        if (time.monotonic() - last_best_time > 25.0 and
                time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0):
            if time.monotonic() - last_lp > 45.0:
                lp_budget = min(40.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20)
                if lp_budget > 10.0:
                    p_lp, s_lp = _lp_refine(p, target_C, lp_budget, seed + 7919 + it)
                    if np.isfinite(s_lp):
                        if s_lp < best_score:
                            best_score = s_lp
                            best = p_lp.copy()
                        p = p_lp.copy()
                else:
                    p = _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.35)
                last_lp = time.monotonic()
            else:
                p = _random_jump(p, best, rng, scale=0.35)
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def _evaluate_safe(p):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.asarray(p).tolist()]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point. Returns a list of non-negative heights.
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    seed_int = int(seed) if seed is not None else 0

    print(f"propose_candidate start seed={seed_int} budget={total_budget:.0f}s", flush=True)

    provided_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    provided = _as_1d_float(provided_raw)
    if provided is not None:
        print(f"provided sequence length={provided.size}", flush=True)

    lengths = [256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]
    if provided is not None:
        pn = int(provided.size)
        if 128 <= pn <= 10000 and pn not in lengths:
            lengths.append(pn)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    if time.monotonic() > start_time + total_budget - 30.0:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cands = _collect_candidates(seed_int, lengths, provided)
    print(f"candidate pool size={len(cands)} (t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s)", flush=True)

    if not cands:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    max_sel = 6 if total_budget > 1200.0 else 4
    selected = _select_candidates(cands, max_sel=max_sel)
    print(f"selected candidates={len(selected)}", flush=True)

    main_deadline = start_time + max(60.0, total_budget - 180.0)
    exact_deadline = start_time + max(30.0, total_budget - 30.0)

    global_best = None
    global_best_score = float("inf")
    best_by_n = {}

    main_budget = max(60.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
    fracs = [0.40, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.07, 0.03][:len(selected)]
    total_frac = sum(fracs) if fracs else 1.0

    for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
        if time.monotonic() > main_deadline - 30.0:
            break

        s0, p0, source = item
        n = int(p0.size)

        start_p = p0
        if n in best_by_n and best_by_n[n][0] < s0:
            start_p = best_by_n[n][1]

        if idx < len(fracs) and total_frac > 0:
            slice_time = main_budget * (fracs[idx] / total_frac)
        else:
            slice_time = max(30.0, main_budget / max(1, len(selected) - idx))

        slice_time = min(slice_time, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if slice_time < 20.0:
            break

        print(
            f"optimizing candidate {idx + 1}/{len(selected)} "
            f"source={source} n={n} initial_fft={s0:.6f} slice={slice_time:.1f}s",
            flush=True
        )

        p_opt, s_opt = _optimize_from_start(start_p, seed_int + 1000 * idx + 17, slice_time, 1.5027)

        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(s_opt)
            global_best = p_opt.copy()

        if n not in best_by_n or (np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_by_n[n][0]):
            best_by_n[n] = (float(s_opt) if np.isfinite(s_opt) else float("inf"), p_opt.copy())

        print(f"  optimized n={n} best_fft={global_best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Exact verification of a few top FFT candidates.
    exact_items = []
    if global_best is not None:
        exact_items.append((global_best_score, global_best))
    for s, p in best_by_n.values():
        if np.isfinite(s):
            exact_items.append((s, p))
    if provided is not None:
        exact_items.append((_fast_score_only(provided), provided.copy()))

    exact_items = [x for x in exact_items if x[1] is not None]
    exact_cands = _unique_candidates(exact_items, limit=5)

    best_exact_seq = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")

    if time.monotonic() < exact_deadline - 10.0:
        for fft_s, p in exact_cands:
            if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline - 5.0:
                break
            val = _evaluate_safe(p)
            print(f"exact verify fft={fft_s:.6f} exact={val:.6f}", flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = float(val)
                best_exact_seq = p.copy()

    if best_exact_seq is None:
        if global_best is not None:
            best_exact_seq = global_best
        else:
            best_exact_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    final = _normalize_positive(best_exact_seq)
    print(f"final n={final.size} best_exact={best_exact_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
